2026-05-28\n128\t\n129\t### Bug Fixes Applied\n130\t\n131\t1. **Boot endpoint fixed** — `chat.js` was calling `/api/netpet/state` (PetDO only, no KV profile). Fixed to `/api/student/state` which returns full KV profile including `teachingFocus`, `primaryBlock`, `lang`, `breakthrough`.\n132\t2. **buildSystemPrompt enriched** — Now injects `teachingFocus` as `TEACHER INSTRUCTION` block + `lang === 'vi'` triggers BILINGUAL MODE (Vietnamese translations after every sentence).\n133\t3. **KV profile fields mapped** — Boot now maps `teachingFocus`, `primaryBlock`, `breakthrough`, `sessionNotes`, `lang`, `streak` from `/api/student/state` into the client-side profile.\n134\t\n135\t### Voice Agent — Low Latency Pipeline\n136\t\n137\t**Architecture:** Browser PCM16 → `/api/agent` WS proxy → Deepgram Voice Agent → DeepSeek LLM → Deepgram TTS → streamed MP3 back.\n138\t\n139\t**Files changed:**\n140\t\n141\t- `src/index.js`: Added `/api/agent` WebSocket proxy handler + `buildAgentPrompt()` helper\n142\t- `public/chat.js`: Added Voice Agent mode (openVoiceAgent, handleAgentMsg, startAgentListening, stopAgentListening, playAgentChunk)\n143\t- REST pipeline kept as fallback when Voice Agent WS unavailable\n144\t\n145\t**DeepSeek config in Voice Agent:**\n146\t\n147\t```json\n148\t{\n149\t  \"think\": {\n150\t    \"provider\": { \"type\": \"open_ai\", \"model\": \"deepseek-chat\", \"temperature\": 0.8 },\n151\t    \"endpoint\": { \"url\": \"https://api.deepseek.com/v1/chat/completions\" }\n152\t  }\n153\t}\n154\t```\n155\t\n156\t**Expected latency:** < 2s vs ~8s with 3-step REST pipeline.\n157\t\n158\t### Portal Mobile Fixes (all 3 portals)\n159\t\n160\t- **Streak badge dismiss button** — Added `×` close button (`.sb-dismiss`) to badge HTML. Streak badge can now be dismissed on mobile.\n161\t- **Hamburger backdrop** — Replaced broken `document.addEventListener('click',...)` click-outside approach with `#navBackdrop` transparent overlay at `z-index:8999`. Tapping backdrop calls `closeNav()`. IDs: `navToggle` (button), `navLinks` (menu), `navBackdrop` (overlay). Applied to all 3 portals.\n162\t\n163\t---\n164\t\n165\t## OBI Voice + Chat Fixes — 2026-05-29\n166\t\n167\t### Root Cause: Serial context fetches killed latency\n168\t\n169\t`handleChat()` in `index.js` had **7 sequential `await` calls** before the DeepSeek call:\n170\tLedger DO → Session DO → Weakness DO → Pet DO → `buildStructuredContext()` (4 more DOs) → Soul logger HTTP → KV reads.\n171\tEach could be 200-500ms. Total: up to 4-8 seconds before the LLM even started.\n172\t\n173\t**Fix:** Collapsed all 7 into a single `Promise.allSettled([...])` round — all fire simultaneously. Time = slowest single fetch, not the sum.\n174\t\n175\tAlso: `rosterStub.fetch()` and `updateStudentMemory()` were both `await`ed AFTER DeepSeek returned. Made fire-and-forget (`.catch(() => {})`).\n176\t\n177\t### Root Cause: Bilingual mode silently dropped\n178\t\n179\t`capContextInjection()` has a 1200-char hard cap. `vietnameseContext` was passed as the 8th argument — ledger/session/weakness filled the budget first, Vietnamese instruction was silently cut every time.\n180\t\n181\t**Fix:** `vietnameseContext` and `teachingFocusContext` now passed FIRST in `capContextInjection` — guaranteed never truncated.\n182\t\n183\t### Root Cause: teachingFocus never reached the server\n184\t\n185\t`handleChat()` only read `lang:<id>` from KV, never the student profile. `teachingFocus` (\"Speak VERY slowly, short sentences...\") was only available client-side, but the server-side prompt REPLACES the client's system prompt.\n186\t\n187\t**Fix:** `handleChat()` now reads `student_profile_<id>` from KV in parallel with `lang:<id>`, extracts `teachingFocus`, injects as `TEACHER INSTRUCTION` block.\n188\t\n189\t### Root Cause: isVague() matched \"anything\" as substring\n190\t\n191\tPattern `/anything|something|everything/` matched inside any sentence containing those words. Student saying \"Anything about health interests me\" triggered the \"Come on! Say something!\" scripted fallback instead of the LLM.\n192\t\n193\t**Fix:** Only match as standalone response: `/^(whatever|anything|something|everything)\\.?\\s*$/`\n194\t\n195\t### Deepgram Voice Agent — Text routing\n196\t\n197\tWhen student types a message (fallback path), it now routes through the open Deepgram Voice Agent WS as `{type: \"TextInput\", text}` instead of hitting `/api/chat` → DeepSeek. Deepgram's in-house LLM responds via `ConversationText` event in ~200-500ms.\n198\t\n199\t- `agentTextResolve` promise pattern: `reactLLM()` sets resolver, `handleAgentMsg` resolves it on assistant `ConversationText`\n200\t- 6-second timeout → falls back to REST if agent doesn't respond\n201\t\n202\t### Voice-First UI: magic-chat.html\n203\t\n204\tApp is a speaking trainer — text input removed from visible UI.\n205\t- `#msg` input and `#sendBtn` hidden via CSS (`display: none`) — kept in DOM so JS refs don't break\n206\t- `#mic` button made large (72×72px), centered, prominent\n207\t- `#micStatus` text element added below mic button — shows: \"Tap to start\" / \"Connecting…\" / \"Listening…\" / \"Processing…\"\n208\t- `#mic.mic-active` pulsing purple animation (existing)\n209\t\n210\t### Auto-mic: gesture-time stream acquisition\n211\t\n212\tBrowser security requires `getUserMedia()` to be called inside a user gesture event. Calling it from a WebSocket `onmessage` handler silently fails even if the user tapped earlier.\n213\t\n214\t**Fix:** On first tap/click/keydown anywhere on the page, `getUserMedia()` is called immediately (inside gesture context). Stream stored in `pendingMicStream`. When `SettingsApplied` fires from Deepgram, the stored stream is connected directly — no second permission request needed.\n215\t\n216\tVoice transcriptions now appear as student chat bubbles (previously dumped into `input.value`).\n217\t\n218\t### Root Cause: Wrong Deepgram Voice Agent URL (SESSION LOOP — STOP REPEATING)\n219\t\n220\tWe were connecting to `https://agent.deepgram.com/` — **wrong URL**. Correct: `https://agent.deepgram.com/v1/agent/converse`. This caused slow/failed connections. Always use the full path.\n221\t\n222\t### Root Cause: Wrong text injection event name (SESSION LOOP — STOP REPEATING)\n223\t\n224\tWe sent `{type: \"TextInput\", text: \"...\"}` — **does not exist** in Deepgram API. Correct: `{type: \"InjectUserMessage\", content: \"...\"}`. Verified from official docs.\n225\t\n226\t### Root Cause: BYO DeepSeek LLM caused unnecessary external hop\n227\t\n228\tWe used `provider.type: \"open_ai\"` + `endpoint.url: deepseek.com` + `DEEPSEEK_API_KEY`. This routes through an external API (China-hosted, variable latency).\n229\t\n230\t**Fix**: Deepgram manages OpenAI, Anthropic, Google, NVIDIA models natively — **no API key needed**. Just set `provider.type: \"open_ai\"` with `model: \"gpt-4o-mini\"` and NO `endpoint` field. Deepgram handles the LLM call on their own infrastructure. Remove all external LLM endpoints from the Voice Agent settings.\n231\t\n232\t### Root Cause: openVoiceAgent() called too late in boot (15-second startup)\n233\t\n234\t`openVoiceAgent()` was called at the END of boot() after 5 serial awaits. Voice Agent connection didn't start until 5+ seconds into page load.\n235\t\n236\t**Fix**: Call `openVoiceAgent()` immediately after `urlStudentId` is extracted (first thing in boot). Also parallelized all 4 boot API calls (state + memory + vocab + hint) with `Promise.allSettled`.\n237\t\n238\t### Root Cause: /api/agent blocked 101 response on Deepgram connection\n239\t\n240\tThe CF Worker was `await fetch(\"agent.deepgram.com\")` BEFORE returning the 101 WebSocket upgrade. Browser was stuck waiting for the Deepgram TCP handshake.\n241\t\n242\t**Fix**: Return 101 to browser immediately, connect to Deepgram via `ctx.waitUntil()` in background. Queue any early browser messages until Deepgram is ready.\n243\t\n244\t### Deepgram Voice Agent — Confirmed Correct Spec (READ BEFORE TOUCHING AGENT CODE)\n245\t\n246\t- **WS URL**: `wss://agent.deepgram.com/v1/agent/converse` (NOT just `/`)\n247\t- **Auth**: `Authorization: Token {DEEPGRAM_API_KEY}` header on the WS upgrade fetch\n248\t- **Settings**: sent after receiving `Welcome` event from server\n249\t- **User text injection**: `{type: \"InjectUserMessage\", content: \"...\"}` (NOT TextInput)\n250\t- **Agent speech injection**: `{type: \"InjectAgentMessage\", content: \"...\"}`\n251\t- **Managed LLMs (no key)**: `provider.type: \"open_ai\"` + `model: \"gpt-4o-mini\"` with NO `endpoint` — Deepgram pays for it\n252\t- **Managed LLMs available**: OpenAI, Anthropic (`claude-4-5-haiku-latest`), Google (`gemini-2.5-flash`), NVIDIA\n253\t- **BYO LLMs require key**: Groq, AWS Bedrock — need your own endpoint + credentials\n254\t- **STT model**: `nova-3` (or `flux-general-en` for newer)\n255\t- **TTS model**: `aura-2-asteria-en`\n256\t- **Client sends audio**: binary PCM16 frames directly over WS\n257\t- **Server events**: `Welcome`, `SettingsApplied`, `ConversationText` (role: user|assistant), `AgentAudioDone`, `UserStartedSpeaking`, binary MP3 chunks\n258\t\n259\t### Deploy History (2026-05-29)\n260\t| Version ID | What changed
